This proxy juggling was added to solve https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/580 The idea is to try to figure out automatically whether the the SEI method signatures refer to any class that requires runtime initialization and if so, then add a special package-visible interface to the proxy and register that package for late initialization. That was the most viable strategy how I could move GraalVM towards making the generated proxy class runtime initialized. \r\n\r\nThere are two tests supposed to cover this:\r\n\r\n* io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.CxfClientTest.clientWithRuntimeInitializedPayload()\r\n* io.quarkiverse.cxf.it.ws.mtom.awt.server.MtomAwtTest\r\n\r\nAs it happens none of them actually covers the case that the SEI method refers a class registered for postponed init through RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem. clientWithRuntimeInitializedPayload relies on appilcation.propeties config \r\n\r\n```\r\nquarkus.cxf.client.clientWithRuntimeInitializedPayload.native.runtime-initialized = true\r\nquarkus.native.additional-build-args=--initialize-at-run-time=io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.rtinit.Operands\\\\,io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.rtinit.Result\r\n```\r\n\r\nwhereas MtomAwtTest covers the RuntimeInitializedPackageBuildItem. \r\nSo a test for this case is missing.\r\n\r\nI'd need to ponder a bit on what we should do. This will fix the cycle when using `cfx` with `aws` extension.\r\n\r\n@ppalaga If the cycle cannot be easily broken in the CXF extension, here is my understanding of the cycle created when using CXF with `amazon-service` or `reactive-pg-client`:\r\n\r\nThe issue arises when other extensions emit `ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem` as part of a BuildStep that directly or indirectly consumes `GeneratedBeanBuildItem`. This often happens because they rely on `BeanRegistrationPhaseBuildItem` to discover injection points of certain proxy instances and emit `ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem` as part of the same build step to avoid having one small build step.\r\n\r\nThe problem with the CXF extension occurs because it emits `GeneratedBeanBuildItem` as part of a build steps chain that consumes `RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem`. The cycle lies in `NativeImageConfigBuildStep\r\n` which consume `ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem` and emit `RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem`.\r\n\r\nI believe the easiest is to break the cycle in this build step.\r\n\r\nhttps://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/blob/5c2df5112331ef170c0b0ad83c84c6f853c2d5d6/core/deployment/src/main/java/io/quarkus/deployment/steps/NativeImageConfigBuildStep.java#L37\r\n\r\nTo resolve this, it may be beneficial to split this build step into two separate build steps: one for List\u003cExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem\u003e extensionSslNativeSupport and another for BuildProducer\u003cRuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem\u003e runtimeInit, BuildProducer\u003cRuntimeReinitializedClassBuildItem\u003e runtimeReinit.\r\n\r\nAlternatively, in order to break the cycle when used with `reactive-pg-client`, you can request Quarkus core to move emission of `ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem` to a separate build step. But it is not very pratical.\r\n\r\nhttps://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/blob/5c2df5112331ef170c0b0ad83c84c6f853c2d5d6/extensions/reactive-pg-client/deployment/src/main/java/io/quarkus/reactive/pg/client/deployment/ReactivePgClientProcessor.java#L108\r\n\r\nWe maybe re-open https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/issues/33955.\r\n","bodyHTML":"\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eHey \u003ca class=\"user-mention notranslate\" data-hovercard-type=\"user\" data-hovercard-url=\"/users/scrocquesel/hovercard\" data-octo-click=\"hovercard-link-click\" data-octo-dimensions=\"link_type:self\" href=\"https://github.com/scrocquesel\"\u003e@scrocquesel\u003c/a\u003e,\u003cbr\u003e\nyes the build and test works fine with your branch! This often happens because they rely on `BeanRegistrationPhaseBuildItem` to discover injection points of certain proxy instances and emit `ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem` as part of the same build step to avoid having one small build step.\r\n\r\nThe problem with the CXF extension occurs because it emits `GeneratedBeanBuildItem` as part of a build steps chain that consumes `RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem`. After thorough pondering about the root cause and the proposed fixes, I am for fixing the issue in the following way:\r\n* We will stop trying to autodetect whether a SEI references a runtime initialized class thus avoiding the \"cycle detected\" failures.\r\n* The only way how to make such SEIs work will be adding `quarkus.cxf.client.myClient.native.runtime-initialized = true` to application.properties. This option exists already as a fall-back for cases uncovered by the imperfect auto-detection. \r\n* I'd prefer avoiding to use `-H:+UseNewExperimentalClassInitialization` for now, while Quarkus CXF and Quarkus are not properly tested with the option enabled.     mavenCentral()
}

    annotation(&quot;jakarta.ws.rs.Path&quot;)
    annotation(&quot;jakarta.persistence.Entity&quot;)
}

    dependsOn(&quot;jandex&quot;)
}

tasks.compileTestKotlin {
    dependsOn(&quot;jandex&quot;)
}

tasks.jacocoTestReport {
    dependsOn(&quot;jandex&quot;)
}

dependencies {
    implementation(&quot;io.quarkiverse.cxf:quarkus-cxf:2.0.2&quot;)
    implementation(project(&quot;:domain&quot;))

plugins {
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.allopen").version("1.8.20")
    id ("org.kordamp.gradle.jandex") version "0.12.0"
    id("io.quarkus")
    id ("java-test-fixtures")
}

repositories {
    mavenCentral()
}

allOpen {
    annotation("jakarta.ws.rs.Path")
    annotation("jakarta.persistence.Entity")
}

tasks.quarkusDependenciesBuild {
    dependsOn("jandex")
}

tasks.compileTestKotlin {
    dependsOn("jandex")
}

tasks.jacocoTestReport {
    dependsOn("jandex")
}

dependencies {
    implementation("io.quarkiverse.cxf:quarkus-cxf:2.0.2")
    implementation(project(":domain"))

    implementation("org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-datetime:0.4.0")

    implementation("io.quarkus:quarkus-liquibase")
    implementation("io.quarkus:quarkus-jdbc-postgresql")

    implementation("com.sun.xml.ws:rt:4.0.1")
    implementation("io.quarkus:quarkus-reactive-pg-client")
    implementation("io.vertx:vertx-lang-kotlin-coroutines:4.4.0")
    implementation("org.ufoss.kotysa:kotysa-vertx-sqlclient:2.4.0")
    implementation("io.smallrye.reactive:mutiny-kotlin:2.2.0")

    val quarkusPlatformGroupId: String by project
    val quarkusPlatformArtifactId: String by project
    val quarkusPlatformVersion: String by project
    implementation(enforcedPlatform("${quarkusPlatformGroupId}:${quarkusPlatformArtifactId}:${quarkusPlatformVersion}"))
    
    testImplementation("io.quarkus:quarkus-junit5")
    testImplementation("io.quarkus:quarkus-jdbc-h2")

    testFixturesApi(enforcedPlatform("${quarkusPlatformGroupId}:${quarkusPlatformArtifactId}:${quarkusPlatformVersion}"))
    testFixturesApi("io.quarkus:quarkus-test-common")
    testFixturesApi("org.testcontainers:cockroachdb:1.17.6")
    testFixturesApi("com.github.tomakehurst:wiremock:2.27.2")
}

actions</span></div></div></div></div></div><div class="Body__scgEjp IssueCommentBody__DGWd7U"><div data-testid="markdown-body" data-team-hovercards-enabled="true" class="markdown-body" data-turbolinks="false"><div class="markdown-body safe-html-box__Uwc6Al Container__ZAxOBo"><p dir="auto">Thanks for the investigation, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/shumonsharif/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/shumonsharif">@shumonsharif</a>! This proxy juggling was added to solve <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="1427418901" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/580" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/580/hovercard" href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/580">#580</a> The idea is to try to figure out automatically whether the the SEI method signatures refer to any class that requires runtime initialization and if so, then add a special package-visible interface to the proxy and register that package for late initialization. That was the most viable strategy how I could move GraalVM towards making the generated proxy class runtime initialized.</p>
<p dir="auto">There are two tests supposed to cover this:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.CxfClientTest.clientWithRuntimeInitializedPayload()</li>
<li>io.quarkiverse.cxf.it.ws.mtom.awt.server.MtomAwtTest</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">As it happens none of them actually covers the case that the SEI method refers a class registered for postponed init through RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem. clientWithRuntimeInitializedPayload relies on appilcation.propeties config</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="quarkus.cxf.client.clientWithRuntimeInitializedPayload.native.runtime-initialized = true
quarkus.native.additional-build-args=--initialize-at-run-time=io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.rtinit.Operands\\,io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.rtinit.Result"><pre class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">quarkus.cxf.client.clientWithRuntimeInitializedPayload.native.runtime-initialized = true
quarkus.native.additional-build-args=--initialize-at-run-time=io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.rtinit.Operands\\,io.quarkiverse.cxf.client.it.rtinit.Result
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">whereas MtomAwtTest covers the RuntimeInitializedPackageBuildItem.<br>
So a test for this case is missing.</p>
<p dir="auto">I'd need to ponder a bit on what we should do.</p></div></div><div role="toolbar" aria-label="Reactions" class="d-flex gap-1 flex-wrap"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></li><li <blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Hey <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/scrocquesel/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/scrocquesel">@scrocquesel</a>,<br>
yes the build and test works fine with your branch! Thanks for your effort and amazing work!</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Good to know, I'll make a release soon.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/knuspertante/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/knuspertante">@knuspertante</a> <code class="notranslate">quarkus-amazon-service</code> 2.3.1 is out. This will fix the cycle when using <code class="notranslate">cfx</code> with <code class="notranslate">aws</code> extension.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/ppalaga/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/ppalaga">@ppalaga</a> If the cycle cannot be easily broken in the CXF extension, here is my understanding of the cycle created when using CXF with <code class="notranslate">amazon-service</code> or <code class="notranslate">reactive-pg-client</code>:</p>
<p dir="auto">The issue arises when other extensions emit <code class="notranslate">ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem</code> as part of a BuildStep that directly or indirectly consumes <code class="notranslate">GeneratedBeanBuildItem</code>. This often happens because they rely on <code class="notranslate">BeanRegistrationPhaseBuildItem</code> to discover injection points of certain proxy instances and emit <code class="notranslate">ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem</code> as part of the same build step to avoid having one small build step.</p>
<p dir="auto">The problem with the CXF extension occurs because it emits <code class="notranslate">GeneratedBeanBuildItem</code> as part of a build steps chain that consumes <code class="notranslate">RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem</code>. The cycle lies in <code class="notranslate">NativeImageConfigBuildStep </code> which consume <code class="notranslate">ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem</code> and emit <code class="notranslate">RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">I believe the easiest is to break the cycle in this build step.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/blob/5c2df5112331ef170c0b0ad83c84c6f853c2d5d6/core/deployment/src/main/java/io/quarkus/deployment/steps/NativeImageConfigBuildStep.java#L37">https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/blob/5c2df5112331ef170c0b0ad83c84c6f853c2d5d6/core/deployment/src/main/java/io/quarkus/deployment/steps/NativeImageConfigBuildStep.java#L37</a></p>
<p dir="auto">To resolve this, it may be beneficial to split this build step into two separate build steps: one for List extensionSslNativeSupport and another for BuildProducer runtimeInit, BuildProducer runtimeReinit.</p>
<p dir="auto">Alternatively, in order to break the cycle when used with <code class="notranslate">reactive-pg-client</code>, you can request Quarkus core to move emission of <code class="notranslate">ExtensionSslNativeSupportBuildItem</code> to a separate build step. But it is not very pratical.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/blob/5c2df5112331ef170c0b0ad83c84c6f853c2d5d6/extensions/reactive-pg-client/deployment/src/main/java/io/quarkus/reactive/pg/client/deployment/ReactivePgClientProcessor.java#L108">https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/blob/5c2df5112331ef170c0b0ad83c84c6f853c2d5d6/extensions/reactive-pg-client/deployment/src/main/java/io/quarkus/reactive/pg/client/deployment/ReactivePgClientProcessor.java#L108</a></p>
<p dir="auto">We maybe re-open <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="1751412789" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/issues/33955" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/quarkusio/quarkus/issues/33955/hovercard" href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/issues/33955">quarkusio/quarkus#33955</a>.</p></div></div><div role="toolbar" aria-label="Reactions" class="d-flex gap-1 flex-wrap"><div here are the cxf chain of build item:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="GeneratedBeanBuildItem
    ServiceSeiBuildItem
        CombinedIndexBuildItem

    CxfBusBuildItem.
   CxfClientBuildItem
        RuntimeInitializedPackageBuildItem
        RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem
        CombinedIndexBuildItem

    ServiceSeiBuildItem
        CombinedIndexBuildItem
    ClientSeiBuildItem
        RuntimeInitializedPackageBuildItem
        RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem
        CombinedIndexBuildItem

    CxfBusBuildItem.
   CxfClientBuildItem

        RuntimeInitializedClassBuildItem
        CombinedIndexBuildItem
        CxfFixedConfig

<p dir="auto">and java2wsdl consume GeneratedBeanBuildItem produce GeneratedResourceBuildItem</p>
<p dir="auto">so I think we can split CxfClientProcessor.collectClients in 2 build step.<br>
For that we will need to split CxfClientBuildItem in 2 parts. one for generateClientProducers on GeneratedBeanBuildItem without proxy and one build item for proxy runtime initialized class="Body__scgEjp IssueCommentBody__DGWd7U"><div data-testid="markdown-body" data-team-hovercards-enabled="true" class="markdown-body" data-turbolinks="false"><div class="markdown-body safe-html-box__Uwc6Al Container__ZAxOBo"><p dir="auto">Sorry for the delay. After thorough pondering about the root cause and the proposed fixes, I am for fixing the issue in the following way:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>We will stop trying to autodetect whether a SEI references a runtime initialized class thus avoiding the "cycle detected" failures.</li>
<li>The only way how to make such SEIs work will be adding <code class="notranslate">quarkus.cxf.client.myClient.native.runtime-initialized = true</code> to application.properties. This option exists already as a fall-back for cases uncovered by the imperfect auto-detection.</li>
<li>I'd prefer avoiding to use <code class="notranslate">-H:+UseNewExperimentalClassInitialization</code> for now, while Quarkus CXF and Quarkus are not properly tested with the option enabled. I'd vote for starting to use it once it becomes default in GraalVM used by Quarkus. After that we can deprecate and remove <code class="notranslate">quarkus.cxf.client.myClient.native.runtime-initialized</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">What do others think?</p></div></div><div role="toolbar" aria-label="Reactions" class="d-flex gap-1 flex-wrap"><div class="ReactionPopoverAnchor__B84pko"><button data-component="Button" data-variant="default" aria-describedby="_R_rda2om3mr9mm5_ _R_rda2om3mr9mm5H2_"><span data-component="buttonContent" data-align="center" class="prc-Button-ButtonContent-Iohp5"><span
